GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
Brass 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
20 to 30 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Outer wall 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Outer wall made of mild steel, inside & lid with SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Swing nut 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Neoprene Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
PID temperature controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes

NA

NA 
 
Unit having a heater fitted at the bottom and with capacity
NA

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
20 to 30 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Outer wall 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Hinged door 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Digital timer controller with END cycle buzzer 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes 
 
Automatic pressure control switch
Yes 
 
Manual Water Filling & Removal
Yes 
 
Unit having a heater fitted at the bottom and with capacity
≥
 3 KW

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
41 to 50 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Hinged door 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Automatic 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes

Yes 
 
Manual Water Filling & Removal
Yes

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
Brass

20 to 30

Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Outer wall

Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Swing nut 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Neoprene Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
PID temperature controller

LCD / LED/ TFT

5 Inches or more

100 to 300

valves

121 to 135

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
81 to 100 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Radial locking 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Elastomeric Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes 
 
Automatic pressure control switch
Yes 
 
Manual Water Filling & Removal
Yes 
 
Unit having a heater fitted at the bottom and with capacity
≥
 6 KW

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
Brass 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
20 to 30 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Outer wall 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Outer wall made of mild steel, inside & lid with SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Swing nut 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Neoprene Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
PID temperature controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz

Yes

Yes

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
81 to 100 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Radial locking 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Elastomeric Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes

Yes

Yes

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
20 to 30 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Outer wall 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Hinged door 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Digital timer controller with END cycle buzzer 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT

5 Inches or more

100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves

121 to 135

15 to 32

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L

51 to 60

Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material

Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Radial locking 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Manual 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT

5 Inches or more

100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry

121 to 135

15 to 32

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
41 to 50 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Radial locking 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Automatic 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes

Yes

Yes

 3 KW

GENERAL FEATURES
Product Name
Vertical Autoclave 
 
Purpose
Vertical Autoclave is used for sterlization / disinfection / pre-treatment of
medical, laboratory, research and surgical tools from all forms of
pathogens, biological organic matter. 
 
PRODUCT
INFORMATION
All piping material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer chamber capacity (usable volume) (Litres)
41 to 50 
 
Sterilizer Chamber Type
Cylindrical 
 
Wall type
Double walled 
 
Sterilizer chamber material
Double walled made of SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Working Pressure of Chamber (PSI)
15 to 20 
 
Type of Sterilizer Chamber Door
Radial locking 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door material
SS 304 / SS 316 / SS 316 L 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door locking facility
Yes 
 
Sterilizer Chamber door operation
Automatic 
 
Door sealing suitable to withstand temperature upto 140 degree celcius &
pressure upto 20-30 psi
By Silicon Rubber Gasket 
 
Controller type
Microprocessor PID Controller 
 
Digital pressure display
LCD / LED/ TFT 
 
Minimum Display Size (Inches)
5 Inches or more 
 
Temperature sensor range (degree celcius)
100 to 300 
 
Audio - visual Alarm facility available for notifying
Low water alarm system as well as steam release valves and safety
valves,Cut off automatically when the autoclave is dry 
 
Working temperature (degree celsius)
121 to 135 
 
Working pressure (PSI)
15 to 32 
 
Power supply
220- 440, three phase/ single phase, 50 - 60 Hz 
 
Print records facility
Yes 
 
Water level indicator
Yes 
 
Water inlet & outlet
Yes

Yes 
 
Manual Water Filling & Removal
Yes

≥
 3 KW
